### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A body of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is moving in a medium and experiences a frictional force <math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>v</mi></math>, where <math><mi>v</mi></math> is the speed of the body. The initial speed is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> and after <math><mn>10</mn></math> s, its energy becomes half of the initial energy. Then, the value of <math><mi>k</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>ln</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>ln</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>20</mn></mfrac><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>20</mn></mfrac><mi>ln</mi><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∵</mo><mi> </mi></math> According to the question,

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi> </mi><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

or <math><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><msub><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>

Given, <math><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>v</mi></math>

or <math><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>v</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>v</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></munderover><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mi> </mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><munderover><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></munderover><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∵</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>k</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow> <mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn> </mn><mi>v</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow> <mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi></mrow> <mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn> 10</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow> <mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn> 10</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow> <mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow> <mi>l</mi><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The displacement of an object of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is given by the relation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></mfrac><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> , where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math> is time in seconds. If the work done by the net force on the object in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>p</mi><mi>q</mi></mfrac><mo> </mo><mi>joule</mi></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>q</mi></math> are smallest integer values, then what is the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi></math>?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **11**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>v</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>S</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>S</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>v</mi><mo>;</mo><mi> </mi></math>
<mi>
<math><mi>W</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>∆</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t=0</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>W=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>m</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⋅</mo><mn>3</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>3 </mn></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mi> </mi><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>11</mn></math>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Initially, a block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is at rest on a frictionless floor and the spring is in a relaxed condition. One end of the spring is rigidly attached to the block and the other end is fixed to a wall. A constant force is applied on the block as shown in the figure. The maximum velocity of the block is

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/efdooidtexniibankgzlhsmrlcibmzycpuatjzshkzurthxzty.png" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>F</mi><msqrt><mi>m</mi><mi>K</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>m</mi><mi>K</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>F</mi><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi>m</mi><mi>K</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>F</mi></mrow><msqrt><mi>m</mi><mi>K</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>F</mi><msqrt><mi>m</mi><mi>K</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/efdooidtexniibankgzlhsmrlcibmzycpuatjzshkzurthxzty.png" />

The block attains maximum speed when it passes through its equilibrium position.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>K</mi><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mi>K</mi></mfrac></math></p><p>From work-energy theorem</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>K</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>K</mi><mi>F</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>F</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mi>K</mi></mfrac></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msup><mi>F</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>K</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mi>F</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>K</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math> is kept on a rough horizontal surface and is attached with a massless spring of force constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math>. The minimum constant force applied on the other end of the spring to lift the block is 

<b><u><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0026924_Question.jpg" style="width: 107px; height: 200px;" /></u></b>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">Mg</mtext><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>M</mi><mi>g</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>)</mo></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 112px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mtext mathvariant="italic">Mg</mtext><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>W</mtext> <mtext>ext</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mo>&#916;</mo> <mtext>E</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msup> <mtext>Kx</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext>&#160;;&#160;</mtext> <mtext>F.x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <msup> <mtext>Kx</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math> or <math> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

For lifting <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>K</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>M</mi><mi>g</mi></math> or <math> <mrow> <mtext>F</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>Mg</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 63px; top: 66.757px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>

</details>

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A ball suspended by a thread, swings in a vertical plane so that its acceleration in the extreme position and lowest position are equal in magnitude. Angle <math><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></math> of thread deflection in the extreme position will be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/xlxjdwdczlvnrusntuwzkojcofaeexvuodhzoaxsohqambiyim.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" />

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mi mathvariant=""> </mi><mi mathvariant="">s</mi><mi mathvariant="">i</mi><mi mathvariant="">n</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi><mi mathvariant=""> </mi></math> (only tangential)

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">l</mi></mrow></mfrac></math> (only radial)

<math><mi mathvariant="italic">K</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">E</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mi mathvariant="italic">P</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">E</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mo>=</mo></math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">m</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">l</mi><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="">m</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">l</mi><mi mathvariant=""> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> ...........(i)

<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">l</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mi mathvariant=""> </mi><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="">c</mi><mi mathvariant="">o</mi><mi mathvariant="">s</mi><mi mathvariant="">θ</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

Since, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">A</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mi mathvariant="">s</mi><mi mathvariant="">i</mi><mi mathvariant="">n</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="italic">g</mi><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="">c</mi><mi mathvariant="">o</mi><mi mathvariant="">s</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⟹</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mo>×</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>⟹</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mo>⟹</mo><mi mathvariant="italic"> </mi><mi mathvariant="italic">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is projected vertically upwards with a speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>16</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>, after some time, when it again passes through the point of projection, its speed is found to be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. It is known that the work done by air resistance is the same during upward and downward motion. Then the maximum height attained by the particle is (Take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>) :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 95px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4.8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>17.6</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>12.8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>8</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math><div id="gtx-trans" style="position: absolute; left: 95px; top: 37.6667px;"><div class="gtx-trans-icon"> </div></div>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>From work-energy theorem 
For upward motion <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>m</mtext> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>mgh</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> </mrow> </math> (work by air resistance)
For downward motion <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>m</mtext> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>mgh</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>W</mtext> <mo>&#8658;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>gh</mtext> </mrow> </math> </p><p>or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A particle is released from a height <math><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> . At certain height its kinetic energy is two times its potential energy. Height and speed of particle at that instant are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> ,  <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> , <math><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> ,  <math><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> ,  <math><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math>  &  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> 

 <math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math> 

 <math><mo>∴</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

</details>

---

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Two particles of masses <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> are initially at rest at an infinite distance apart. If they approach each other under their mutual interaction given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>. Their speed of approach at the instant when they are at a distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></math> apart is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msqrt></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msqrt></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></msqrt></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></msqrt></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msqrt></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#956;</mi></math> be the reduced mass of the system and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi></math> be the approach speed of the particles, then</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msup><mi>&#956;v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></msubsup><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mo>&#8747;</mo><mo>&#8734;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></msubsup><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mi>dr</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mfrac><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The potential energy of a particle under a conservative force is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>U</mi><mfenced><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>. The equilibrium position of the particle is at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>. The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mi>x</mi></math> will be

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **15**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
For a conservative field, Force, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">U</mi><mo>⁡</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>⁡</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></math>

At equilibrium position, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mo>⇒</mo><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>5</mn><mspace width="0.5em"></mspace><mtext>m</mtext></math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>M</mi></math> slides on a frictionless surface with an initial speed of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math>. On top of the block, there is a small block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>m</mi></math>. The coefficient of friction between the blocks is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>μ</mi></math>. The system of blocks encounters an ideal spring with force constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math>. The maximum value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>k</mi></math> for which the blocks don't slip relative to each other is</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0032412_Question1.jpg" style="width: 250px; height: 84px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>m</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>M</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#x3BC;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfenced><msup><mspace/><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><msup><mspace/><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>M</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>m</mi></mrow><mi>k</mi></mfrac></msqrt></math></p><p>Also for the upper block</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>f</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>a</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>m</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>m</mi><mfrac><mi>k</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>k</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow><mi>k</mi></mfrac></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mi>k</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>g</mi></mrow><msub><mi>v</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>M</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A block of mass <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.1</mo><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> is released from a height of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> on a curved smooth surface. On the horizontal surface, path <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AB</mi></math> is smooth and path <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>BC</mi></math> is rough with a coefficient of friction <math><mi>μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.1</mn></math>. If the impact of the block with the vertical wall at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is perfectly elastic, the total distance covered by the block on the horizontal surface before coming to rest will be (take <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>)

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/348114.JPG" style="width: 250px; height: 116px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>29</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>49</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>59</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>109</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#xA0;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>59</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Total <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>KE</mi></math> dissipated against friction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>mgh</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

Work done against friction in one trip across <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>BC</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>mg&#956;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.2</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Total number of trips across <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>BC</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>4</mn><mtext>0.2</mtext></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> Total distance <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>BC</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>19</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>AB</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>59</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The potential energy of a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>kg</mi></math> particle, free to move along the <em>x</em>-axis, is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>V</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>4</mn></msup><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>)</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>J</mtext></math>. The total mechanical energy of the particle is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math> then, the maximum speed (in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>/</mo> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>/</mo> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>/</mo> <msqrt> <mn>2</mn> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Total energy <em>E<sub>T</sub></em> =2 J It is fixed. For maximum speed. kinetic energy is maximum The potential energy should therefore be minimum

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8757;</mo> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mo>(</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>)</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>4</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>  

or <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow></mfrac>&#160;<mo>=</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>(</mo> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>)</mo>&#160; </mrow> </math>

For <em>V</em> to be minimum,<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>d</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>  

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>(</mo> <msup> <mi>x</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>)</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mi>,</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>or&#160;</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mi>,</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mo>&#177;</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>  

At <em>x</em>=0, <em>V</em>(<em>x</em>)=0

At <math> <mrow> <mi>x</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mo>&#177;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mi>,</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> <mo>(</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>)</mo> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>J</mtext> </mrow> </math>  

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#8756;</mo> <mo>(</mo> <mtext>Kinetic energy</mtext> <msub> <mo>)</mo> <mtext>max</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>T</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> </msub> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mrow> <mi></mi><mi></mi><mi>V</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo> </mrow> <mtext>min</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>  

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>(</mo><mtext>Kinetic energy</mtext><msub><mo>)</mo><mtext>max</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mtext>J</mtext></mrow></math> 

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>m</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>9</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math> 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mtext>m</mtext><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mtext>m</mtext><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mi>v</mi><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A massless string and a spring connect two blocks A and B to each other. Block B slides over a frictionless inclined plane while block A slides over horizontal surface. Coefficient of friction between block A horizontal surface is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">μ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.2.</mn></math>At the instant shown blocks are moving with constant speed. Mass of block A and energy stored in spring are respectively. <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>m</mtext><mo>/</mo><msup> <mtext>s</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mtext> </mtext><mo>,</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>m</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>kg</mtext></mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow> </math>

<img alt="" height="149" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ci/image10-a731e98e-ee4d-4dc5-95b0-1839427f59c8.jpg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" width="224" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0.05</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0.05</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>0.05</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&theta;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&mu;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&rArr;   </mo> <msub> <mtext>m</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>g </mtext> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mi>&theta;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>sin</mtext> <mi>&theta;</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mi>&mu;</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>kg</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>kx</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1 </mn> <mtext>cm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&rArr;   </mo> <mtext>Energy</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>&times;</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>.</mtext> <mn>0</mn> <mn>5 </mn> <mtext>Joule</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>
